- **bmad-multimodel**: Decides who runs each subagent that a skill or workflow launches (BMad implementers, review lenses, critics, or any other). A role with a definition file runs on the agent and model that file names (Claude, Gemini through Antigravity, Codex, Gemini CLI, OpenCode); a role without one runs as usual. Use before launching, spawning or starting any subagent, and before re-engaging one. ([MaximilianoFelice/bmad-multimodel](https://github.com/MaximilianoFelice/bmad-multimodel/tree/HEAD/skills/bmad-multimodel))

- **bmc**: Business Model Canvas (Osterwalder & Pigneur) — visualize, design, and pivot business models using 9 building blocks. Includes Value Proposition Canvas for customer fit analysis. ([ironyjk/strategy-frameworks](https://github.com/ironyjk/strategy-frameworks/tree/HEAD/bmc) · ★40)
- **bmc**: Business Model Canvas (Osterwalder): maps a business across the 9 blocks with a value proposition deep dive, delivering a completed BMC with VPC overlay and revenue model analysis. TRIGGER: "business model canvas", "BMC", "modelo de negócio", "desenha o canvas", "value proposition canvas", "revenue model", "/strat bmc". SKIP: testing whether an idea has real demand -&gt; saas/validate-idea (runs validation experiments, not canvas mapping); defining how to stand against competitors -&gt; strat/position. ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/plugins/arkaos-strategy/skills/bmc) · ★18)
- **bmc**: สร้าง Business Model Canvas — Use when user needs a BMC with 9 blocks and analysis. ([kongnakornna/work-book](https://github.com/kongnakornna/work-book/tree/HEAD/AI-Assisted-Development/sa/13-bmc))
- **bmc**: Generate a Business Model Canvas (Alexander Osterwalder / Strategyzer) as HTML or PowerPoint (.pptx) with pixel-accurate layout, color-coded sections, and print-to-PDF support. Use when the user wants to create, visualize, or export a business model canvas. ([terdia/design-skill](https://github.com/terdia/design-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/bmc))
- **bmc-ai-assisted-draft-starting**: Starts a BMC session from an AI-generated first-draft canvas and brand audit instead of a blank page — the team's real work becomes editing, rejecting, and enriching the draft rather than generating it from nothing, which is faster and surfaces disagreement earlier. ([Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer](https://github.com/Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer/tree/HEAD/specialisation-packs/business-model-canvas/skills/bmc-ai-assisted-draft-starting) · ★3)

- **bmc-ai-scaled-customer-interviewing**: Uses AI-moderated interviews to run customer discovery conversations at a scale no human team could match — hundreds of short interviews in parallel — then treats the aggregate as a filter: mines it for the most interesting outliers and patterns, and follows up on those personally. ([Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer](https://github.com/Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer/tree/HEAD/specialisation-packs/business-model-canvas/skills/bmc-ai-scaled-customer-interviewing) · ★3)

- **bmc-antibody-and-sandbox-design**: Reads a new business model for organizational-rejection triggers — margin conflict, sales-process conflict, cannibalization fear — inside the client's own organization, and designs a protected sandbox so internal 'antibodies' don't kill the experiment before it's tested. ([Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer](https://github.com/Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer/tree/HEAD/specialisation-packs/business-model-canvas/skills/bmc-antibody-and-sandbox-design) · ★3)
- **bmc-antipattern-and-misunderstanding-correction**: Identifies five of the expert's own BMC-work antipatterns (e.g. too much content per block, too deep too early, canvas as a strategy document) and four of the most common client misunderstandings about the BMC's role, and offers direct corrective moves for each. ([Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer](https://github.com/Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer/tree/HEAD/specialisation-packs/business-model-canvas/skills/bmc-antipattern-and-misunderstanding-correction) · ★3)
- **bmc-build**: Construct the initial Business Model Canvas from venture vision and customer-discovery state. Tags every cell as hypothesis or fact. Writes 05-business-model/bmc-vN.md (or 01-hypotheses/bmc-v1.md). - **bmc-canvas-clarity-and-iteration**: Guides BMC work with a variation logic aimed at clarity over depth: builds 2-3 alternative canvas variants, identifies when a team is stuck on a single canvas, and assesses canvas readiness using the expert's three criteria. ([Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer](https://github.com/Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer/tree/HEAD/specialisation-packs/business-model-canvas/skills/bmc-canvas-clarity-and-iteration) · ★3)
- **bmc-canvas-diagnostic-reading**: Reads a finished BMC canvas as a diagnostic instrument using seven research-based rules (Hook Rule, value-cost consistency, segment specificity, evidence grade, defensive canvas, missing block, gravity/lock-in check) and a four-dimension quality rubric. ([Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer](https://github.com/Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer/tree/HEAD/specialisation-packs/business-model-canvas/skills/bmc-canvas-diagnostic-reading) · ★3)
- **bmc-channel-economics-check**: Puts a real cost-to-acquire against every Channel on the canvas and checks it against the company's actual budget, payback period, and cash runway — so channels get chosen on economics, not just descriptive fit or trend appeal. ([Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer](https://github.com/Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer/tree/HEAD/specialisation-packs/business-model-canvas/skills/bmc-channel-economics-check) · ★3)
- **bmc-client-language-translation**: Interprets typical client statements about BMC work ('we need to update our business model', 'we've already done a BMC') as strategic signals, and corrects three of the most common conceptual misunderstandings: value proposition as a feature list, customer segment as demographics, revenue stream as pricing. ([Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer](https://github.com/Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer/tree/HEAD/specialisation-packs/business-model-canvas/skills/bmc-client-language-translation) · ★3)
- **bmc-consistency-checker**: 商业模式画布一致性检查器 - 验证商业模式各模块的交叉一致性： 1. 全局考虑：覆盖BMC 9(+1)个模块的交叉验证 2. 系统考虑：输入→交叉检查→问题识别→建议生成闭环 3. 迭代机制：根据实际运营反馈优化检查规则 4. Skill化：标准接口，可嵌入任何商业模式设计流程 5. 流程自动化：自动检查并生成一致性报告 ([egbertie/satisficing-institute](https://github.com/egbertie/satisficing-institute/tree/HEAD/skills/.archive_bmc-consistency-checker))

Triggers on phrases like "measure on the Deck", "RssAnon against master", "compare with master on the device", "baseline before deploying", "startup time on the Deck", or "is it slower on the device". - **bmc-dsl**: The `.bmc` file format of the Business Model Canvas Modeler: a YAML map with `version`, `canvas` (its title and notes) and the nine building blocks, each holding `items` (id, text, color, notes) and block `notes`, plus quoting, parser diagnostics, the canonical form and the round-trip guarantee. Use when reading, writing, editing or validating a `.bmc` file or canvas text; for the Business Model Canvas method and reviews use the `business-model-canvas` skill. - **bmc-economic-prototyping**: Forces pricing, unit economics, and revenue-model assumptions into a live, testable form at the start of BMC work — a dynamic numbers prototype and a willingness-to-pay test — instead of leaving Revenue Streams and Cost Structure as an afterthought. - **bmc-experiment-method-selection**: Decides HOW to test a BMC hypothesis based on real build effort — build and test immediately if a working version is achievable in about two weeks, otherwise choose a cheaper proxy method (landing page, clickable mockup, Wizard of Oz, pre-order) instead of starting to build. - **bmc-proxy-expert-validation**: Finds people who professionally interact with your target customer's Jobs, Pains, and Gains every day — salespeople, support staff, brokers, consultants adjacent to your category — and interviews them as a fast, cheap, pattern-rich validation source before or alongside direct customer interviews. - **bmc-review-addressing**: Work through inbound code-review feedback and CI failures on an MR/PR branch, one item at a time with the user in the loop — the counterpart to authoring a review (that's `code-review`). Cover… ([BraiinsForge/bmc-main](https://github.com/BraiinsForge/bmc-main/tree/HEAD/.ai/skills/bmc-review-addressing) · ★31)
- **bmc-self-review**: Use when reviewing your own committed branch in this repository before offering it for review or opening an MR — when the `bmc-finalizing-ticket` skill offers it, or when asked to. Triggers on "self-review the branch", "review my branch", "review the branch", "is this ready", "check my changes". ([BraiinsForge/bmc-main](https://github.com/BraiinsForge/bmc-main/tree/HEAD/.ai/skills/bmc-self-review) · ★31)
- **bmc-sensemaking-question-mapping**: Builds the BMC as a structured set of open, falsifiable questions per block instead of filled-in answers, and distinguishes 'we lack data' gaps from 'we haven't interpreted contradictory signals' gaps — shifting effort from collecting more information (cheap, AI-abundant) to asking better questions (the actual scarce skill). ([Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer](https://github.com/Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer/tree/HEAD/specialisation-packs/business-model-canvas/skills/bmc-sensemaking-question-mapping) · ★3)
- **bmc-session-facilitation-design**: Designs the structure of a BMC workshop using research-based heuristics: choosing a starting point, fill-in order, session length/team composition, when the canvas isn't yet finished, evidence color-coding, and presenting the canvas to stakeholders. ([Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer](https://github.com/Pilot2Service/AI-Business-Designer/tree/HEAD/specialisation-packs/business-model-canvas/skills/bmc-session-facilitation-design) · ★3)
- **bmc-spec**: Looks up what a BMC specification or the OpenBMC, Aspeed or Nuvoton source says and answers with a citation: document, version, section and page, or repository, commit and line. Use it before answering any question about a BMC protocol, command, field, value or register, even when the answer seems known: an answer from memory has no citation and may be wrong; never answer from memory and offer the lookup afterwards. The Source Catalog holds over a hundred documents: IPMI and DCMI, the DMTF families (MCTP, PLDM, SPDM, NC-SI, SMBIOS, Redfish), OData, NVMe and NVMe-MI, OCP DC-SCM and DC-MHS, I2C… ([ianmhuang/bmc-toolkit](https://github.com/ianmhuang/bmc-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/bmc-spec))

Use when the user requests 'validate wave', 'wave validation', 'run milestone', 'close milestone', 'differential test wave', 'rollback verification', or the orchestrator routes to the VALIDATE or MILESTONE state for a wave. ([guercheLE/bmad-bmf](https://github.com/guercheLE/bmad-bmf/tree/HEAD/skills/bmf-validate))

Use available BMH code or a verified CAD engine; do not imply that this skill installs or connects FreeCAD. Include a read-only snapshot checker; do not certify structural or regulatory compliance. - **bmh-interior-exterior-3d**: Build, edit, and verify synchronized interior and exterior 3D scenes for Build My Home (BMH), including rooms, stairs, doors, furniture, materials, lighting, camera views, Blender renders, and web GLB assets. - **bmh-visual-quality**: Improve actual Build My Home (BMH) visualization against user photos, videos or render references. Apply to code and real scene output. ([hui-minh-bach/ai-project-os-source](https://github.com/hui-minh-bach/ai-project-os-source/tree/HEAD/integrations/skill-snapshots/bmh-visual-quality) · ★4)

- **bmw-design-analysis**: BMW's corporate site — distinct from BMW M's motorsport-bombastic variant, this is a measured and settled corporate-automotive interface. On a light (cream-tinted white) canvas, BMW corporate blue (#1c69d4) carries every primary CTA; dark navy hero bands frame model photography. BMW Type Next Latin sets the entire hierarchy on two weights — heavy 700 display and Light 300 body. Configuration and reservation flows ride a card-based 4-up grid, where each card holds a model render, a name, and a "Learn More" link. - **bn-ask**: Grounded codebase Q&A via a dedicated research subtree. Use when the user asks how this repo works, where behavior lives, whether a hypothesis about the code is true, what limitations or unknowns exist, or wants orientation in unfamiliar code. Read-only; always opens a run and dispatches researchers plus an answer-checker, then answers with evidence, confidence, and explicit uncertainty. For a throwaway question, ask in plain chat instead. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/bn-ask) · 2 copies)
- **bn-brainstorm**: Explore requirements and approaches through collaborative dialogue, then write a right-sized requirements document. Use when the user says "let's brainstorm", "what should we build", or "help me think through X", presents a vague or ambitious feature request, or seems unsure about scope or direction -- even without explicitly asking to brainstorm. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/bn-brainstorm) · 2 copies)
- **bn-company-formation**: How to incorporate a private company (Sendirian Berhad, Sdn Bhd) in Brunei Darussalam in 2026 through the One Common Portal (OCP) of the Registry of Companies and Business Names (ROCBN). Covers entity choice, the minimum BND 25,000 authorised share capital, two to fifty shareholders, at least two directors with the ordinarily-resident rule, Form X and director consents, the register of controllers, payment, the certificate of incorporation, and the tax and TAP registrations that follow. Use for questions about company formation in Brunei, Sdn Bhd registration, ROCBN, OCP or setting up a busin… ([openaccountants/openaccountants](https://github.com/openaccountants/openaccountants/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/bn-company-formation) · ★498)
- **bn-conventions**: Banyan's run-ledger, delegation-envelope, and knowledge-store conventions; consult to open a new run, inspect or resume a run ledger, write a progress note or artifact, or look up the .banyan/solutions schema. - **bn-corporate-income-tax**: Brunei corporate income tax for year of assessment 2026 (preceding-year basis, so income of the accounting period ending in 2025). Covers the 18.5% rate, the section 35(4) tax threshold for existing companies, the section 35(5) exemption for newly incorporated companies, allowable and disallowed deductions, capital allowances, tax credits, estimated chargeable income, the 30 June return, payment and late payment penalties, and withholding tax on payments to non-residents. - **bn-datacenter-methodology**: Brunei Darussalam (BN) datacenter discovery & audit methodology — how to enumerate, verify, and update Brunei datacenter projects at district granularity (4 districts in the current manifest: Brunei-Muara, Belait, Tutong, Temburong). Brunei is a tiny state-led market: no public national datacenter registry; enumeration joins AITI licensing, EGNC government data-centre pages, UNN/DST/imagine/BIG operator pages, submarine cable landing records (Tungku, Telisai), DES/Berakas Power energy records, procurement, cloud-provider region pages (all negative for BN), and trade press. - **bn-debug**: Distributed debugging via a lead-owned subtree: reproduce, rank hypotheses, test them in parallel with fresh-context investigators, confirm the causal chain, then choose Fix now / Diagnosis only / Rethink design. - **bn-doctor**: Capability doctor: checks the host environment (Claude Code &gt;= 2.1.172, node, gh, python3, pwsh), verifies plugin assets are discoverable and well-formed, and runs a LIVE depth-2 nested-spawn probe plus allowlist, transcript locate+complete, and nested user-question probes. Prints a green/yellow/red checklist and leaves no files behind. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/bn-doctor) · 2 copies)
- **bn-evolve**: Meta-level self-improvement: mine accumulated Banyan run data for recurring harness failures and write evidence-cited, human-applied proposals to improve Banyan's own agents and skills -- never self-applies. Object-level counterpart: /bn-learn. Exploratory; needs ~5+ accumulated runs. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/tree/HEAD/dist/codex/skills/bn-evolve) · 2 copies)
- **bn-fit-modify**: Guidance for Bayesian Network DAG structure recovery, parameter learning, and causal intervention tasks. This skill should be used when tasks involve recovering DAG structure from observational data, learning Bayesian Network parameters, performing causal interventions (do-calculus), or generating samples from modified networks. Applies to tasks mentioning Bayesian networks, DAG recovery, structure learning, causal inference, or interventional distributions. - **bn-grow**: Hands-off end-to-end feature pipeline: open a run ledger, optionally run brainstorm intake for fuzzy ideas, then research -&gt; spec stress -&gt; plan (with a judge panel) -&gt; deliver (which now runs the full reviewer panel and a bounded review-&gt;fix loop on its own integrated result), each owned by its own subtree, with explicit artifact gates and bounded self-recovery before user escalation. Finishes at a ship gate (push stays yours) and a background knowledge-curation dispatch. The trunk stays small -- it holds intent and reads artifacts, the subtrees do the work. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/bn-grow) · 2 copies)
- **bn-hello**: Confirms Banyan is installed and prints its version. Use to verify the plugin loaded, skill dispatch works, and the manifest is readable. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/tree/HEAD/dist/codex/skills/bn-hello) · 2 copies)
- **bn-income-tax**: Brunei personal income tax for 2026 (year of assessment 2026). Brunei levies no tax on the income of individuals, sole proprietorships or partnerships registered as business names, from employment, self-employment, dividends, interest or capital gains. Explains what an individual still pays (SPK retirement contributions for citizens and permanent residents), why there is no PAYE or personal return, and the sole proprietor or company decision. Use for questions like do I pay income tax in Brunei, Brunei salary tax, expat tax in Brunei, sole proprietor tax Brunei or personal tax return Brunei D… ([openaccountants/openaccountants](https://github.com/openaccountants/openaccountants/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/bn-income-tax) · ★498)
- **bn-learn**: Object-level memory: consolidate staged candidate lessons into the .banyan/solutions knowledge store via bn-knowledge-curator (dedup, merge, promote with causal claims gated on tested evidence, mark stale) -- curates what Banyan learned about your codebase. Meta-level counterpart: /bn-evolve. Runs manually, or dispatched in the background at the end of a run (e.g. by /bn-grow). ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/tree/HEAD/dist/codex/skills/bn-learn) · 2 copies)
- **bn-mock**: Turn an idea — free text, a .banyan/brainstorms/*-requirements.md, or a .banyan/plans/*-plan.md — into a deliberately-fake, semi-functional mock under a disposable top-level mock/&lt;slug&gt;/, so design holes surface before an MVP is committed. A thin dispatcher: it resolves the input, opens or reuses a run, runs an overwrite-safety check, spawns exactly one bn-mock-builder leaf under an anti-real-machine fidelity boundary, reads the builder's mock-notes gate artifact, prints the per-medium run command, and routes findings back through the owning skills without ever editing a protected artifact. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/bn-mock) · 2 copies)
- **bn-mockup**: Build a Bristlenose UI mockup. Two modes — bn-accurate (the shipped design system, real --bn-* tokens + real component classes, artefact walled off from commentary) and quick/loose (hand-rolled tokens, fast). Use bn-accurate when the user asks for a "bn accurate" / "design-system-true" / "real pixels" mockup of a Bristlenose surface, or is reviewing a design to build; use quick for a fast sketch. Either beats a long text-only back-and-forth. ([cassiocassio/bristlenose](https://github.com/cassiocassio/bristlenose/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/bn-mockup) · ★7)
- **bn-onboard**: Brownfield repository onboarding into the Banyan shape: discover legacy docs, classify them, gate linked derivatives, stage knowledge through harvest and curate, draft instruction files with approval, and write a stable onboarding manifest. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/tree/HEAD/dist/codex/skills/bn-onboard) · 2 copies)
- **bn-payroll-social**: Brunei payroll and retirement contributions for 2026 under the National Retirement Scheme (Skim Persaraan Kebangsaan, SPK), which replaced the TAP and SCP contribution rates from the July 2023 contribution. - **bn-plan**: Thin dispatcher for durable implementation planning. Captures the task or input artifact, sends one envelope to bn-plan-lead, reads the lead's report, and relays the plan path plus assumed-requirement confirmations. The lead owns run setup, the planning panel, synthesis, ledger updates, and the plan doc. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/tree/HEAD/dist/codex/skills/bn-plan) · 3 copies)
- **bn-poc**: Prove whether an idea's central IP/capability can actually work — free text, a .banyan/brainstorms/*-requirements.md, or a .banyan/plans/*-plan.md — by building its core machine FOR REAL into a disposable top-level poc/&lt;slug&gt;/, skipping the surface, and answering one feasibility question. A thin dispatcher: it resolves the input, opens or reuses a run, runs an overwrite-safety check, confirms a build scope with the user (the crux, a budget with a unit and basis, and the named installs/hosts/data — the cliff that grants execution authority), spawns exactly one code-executing bn-poc-builder lea… ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/tree/HEAD/dist/codex/skills/bn-poc) · 2 copies)
- **bn-release**: Orchestrate a release across all channels — TestFlight, .dmg, PyPI, GitHub Release, Homebrew, Snap, App Store. Runs the mechanical preflight, drafts the changelog and website updates against the diff, then executes in irreversibility order. Wraps /new-release rather than replacing it — that skill remains the CLI-publish specialist and owns the tag-surgery knowledge. ([cassiocassio/bristlenose](https://github.com/cassiocassio/bristlenose/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/bn-release) · ★7)
- **bn-resolve-pr**: Resolve PR review feedback: evaluate validity, fix issues with parallel resolver agents (local edits only), then validate, commit, push, reply, and resolve threads from the trunk. Use when addressing PR review comments, resolving review threads, or fixing code review feedback. Trunk-level, foreground only -- it pushes and posts to GitHub. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/bn-resolve-pr) · 2 copies)
- **bn-review**: Structured READ-ONLY review of a code change via a lead-owned subtree that selects reviewers and dedupes findings, returning a findings report (review-verdict.md + findings/merged.json).
